        <div class="subd" id="stat.524.6-213.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/524/full#stat.524.6-213.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Contract of deposit; generally.</span></h2>
          <p>A contract of deposit that does not contain provisions in substantially the form provided in subdivision 3 is governed by the provisions of this part applicable to the type of account that most nearly conforms to the depositor's intent.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-213.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/619/#laws.0.15.0">1973 c 619 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/603/#laws.0.28.0">1984 c 603 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/292/#laws.0.21.0">1985 c 292 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/3/#laws.1.63.0">1Sp1986 c 3 art 1 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/13/#laws.2.1.0">1993 c 13 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/472/#laws.0.63.0">1994 c 472 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/36/#laws.0.7.0">2013 c 36 s 7</a>,8</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-214">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-214 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-201">524.6-201</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-214">524.6-214</a> may be cited as the "Minnesota Multiparty Accounts Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-214.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/619/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 619 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.2.1.0">1987 c 384 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/472/#laws.0.63.0">1994 c 472 s 63</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-215">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-215 DESIGNATION OF AGENT.</h1>
        <p>(a) By a writing signed by all parties, or by less than all of the parties if the contract of deposit expressly so provides, the parties may designate, as an agent of all parties on an account, a person other than a party.</p>
        <p>(b) Unless the terms of an agency designation provide that the authority of the agent terminates on disability or incapacity of a party, the agent's authority survives disability and incapacity. The agent may act for a party until the authority of the agent is terminated by the party, by an attorney-in-fact appointed by the party, or by a conservator appointed to protect the interests of the party.</p>
        <p>(c) Death of the sole party or last surviving party terminates the authority of an agent.</p>
        <p>(d) Except as otherwise provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-211">524.6-211</a>, a financial institution is not liable for account transactions performed at the direction of, or authorized by, an agent under an agency designation for an account if:</p>
        <p>(1) the financial institution has no actual notice of the termination of the agent's authority before the transaction;</p>
        <p>(2) the financial institution has no actual knowledge of the death of the sole party or last surviving party; or</p>
        <p>(3) the agent's authority does not survive the disability or incapacity of all the parties, and the financial institution has not received actual notice of the disability or incapacity.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-215.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/36/#laws.0.9.0">2013 c 36 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-216">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-216 TYPES OF ACCOUNT; EXISTING ACCOUNTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) An account may be for a single party or multiple parties. A multiple-party account may be with or without a right of survivorship between the parties. Subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-204">524.6-204</a>, either a single-party account or a multiple-party account may have a P.O.D. designation, an agency designation, or both.</p>
        <p>(b) An account established before, on, or after August 1, 2013, whether in the form prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-213">524.6-213</a> or in any other form acceptable to the financial institution, is either a single-party account or a multiple-party account, with or without right of survivorship, and with or without a P.O.D. designation or an agency designation within the meaning of this chapter, and is governed by this chapter.</p>
        <p>(c) An agency designation created on or after August 1, 2013, is governed by this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-216.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/36/#laws.0.10.0">2013 c 36 s 10</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">Part 3<br>UNIFORM TOD SECURITY REGISTRATION ACT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-301">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-301 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <p>In sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a>:</p>
        <p>(1) "Beneficiary form" means a registration of a security which indicates the present owner of the security and the intention of the owner regarding the person who will become the owner of the security upon the death of the owner.</p>
        <p>(2) "Register," including its derivatives, means to issue a certificate showing the ownership of a certificated security or, in the case of an uncertificated security, to initiate or transfer an account showing ownership of securities.</p>
        <p>(3) "Registering entity" means a person who originates or transfers a security title by registration, and includes a broker maintaining security accounts for customers and a transfer agent or other person acting for or as an issuer of securities.</p>
        <p>(4) "Security" means a share, participation, or other interest in property, in a business, or in an obligation of an enterprise or other issuer, and includes a certificated security, an uncertificated security, and a security account.</p>
        <p>(5) "Security account" means (i) a reinvestment account associated with a security, a securities account with a broker, a cash balance in a brokerage account, cash, cash equivalents, interest, earnings, or dividends earned or declared on a security in an account, a reinvestment account, or a brokerage account, whether or not credited to the account before the owner's death, (ii) an investment management or custody account with a trust company or a trust division of a bank with trust powers, including the securities in the account, a cash balance in the account, and cash, cash equivalents, interest, earnings, or dividends earned or declared on a security in the account, whether or not credited to the account before the owner's death, or (iii) a cash balance or other property held for or due to the owner of a security as a replacement for or product of an account security, whether or not credited to the account before the owner's death.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-301.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.1.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/15/#laws.0.12.0">2001 c 15 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-302">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-302 REGISTRATION IN BENEFICIARY FORM; SOLE OR JOINT TENANCY OWNERSHIP.</h1>
        <p>Only individuals whose registration of a security shows sole ownership by one individual or multiple ownership by two or more with right of survivorship, rather than as tenants in common, may obtain registration in beneficiary form. Multiple owners of a security registered in beneficiary form hold as joint tenants with right of survivorship, as tenants by the entireties, or as owners of community property held in survivorship form, and not as tenants in common.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-302.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.2.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-303">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-303 REGISTRATION IN BENEFICIARY FORM; APPLICABLE LAW.</h1>
        <p>A security may be registered in beneficiary form if the form is authorized by this or a similar statute of the state of organization of the issuer or registering entity, the location of the registering entity's principal office, the office of its transfer agent or its office making the registration, or by this or a similar statute of the law of the state listed as the owner's address at the time of registration. A registration governed by the law of a jurisdiction in which this or similar legislation is not in force or was not in force when a registration in beneficiary form was made is nevertheless presumed to be valid and authorized as a matter of contract law.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-303.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.3.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-304">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-304 ORIGINATION OF REGISTRATION IN BENEFICIARY FORM.</h1>
        <p>A security, whether evidenced by certificate or account, is registered in beneficiary form when the registration includes a designation of a beneficiary to take the ownership at the death of the owner or the deaths of all multiple owners.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-304.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.4.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-305">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-305 FORM OF REGISTRATION IN BENEFICIARY FORM.</h1>
        <p>Registration in beneficiary form may be shown by the words "transfer on death" or the abbreviation "TOD," or by the words "pay on death" or the abbreviation "POD," after the name of the registered owner and before the name of a beneficiary.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-305.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.5.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-306">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-306 EFFECT OF REGISTRATION IN BENEFICIARY FORM.</h1>
        <p>The designation of a TOD beneficiary on a registration in beneficiary form has no effect on ownership until the owner's death. A registration of a security in beneficiary form may be canceled or changed at any time by the sole owner or all then surviving owners without the consent of the beneficiary.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-306.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.6.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-307">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-307 DEATH OF OWNER; CREDITORS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.524.6-307.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/524/full#stat.524.6-307.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Ownership on death of owner.</span></h2>
          <p>On death of a sole owner or the last to die of all multiple owners, ownership of securities registered in beneficiary form passes to the beneficiary or beneficiaries who survive all owners. On proof of death of all owners and compliance with any applicable requirements of the registering entity, a security registered in beneficiary form may be reregistered in the name of the beneficiary or beneficiaries who survive the death of all owners. Until division of the security after the death of all owners, multiple beneficiaries surviving the death of all owners hold their interests as tenants in common. If no beneficiary survives the death of all owners, the security belongs to the estate of the deceased sole owner or the estate of the last to die of all multiple owners.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.524.6-307.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/524/full#stat.524.6-307.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Rights of creditors.</span></h2>
          <p>A registration in beneficiary form is not effective against an estate of a deceased sole owner or a deceased last to die of multiple owners to transfer to a beneficiary or beneficiaries sums needed to pay debts, taxes, and expenses of administration, including statutory allowances to the surviving spouse, minor children, and dependent children, if other assets of the estate are insufficient. A TOD beneficiary in whose name a security is registered after the death of the owner is liable to account to the deceased owner's personal representative for securities so registered or their proceeds to the extent necessary to discharge such claims and charges remaining unpaid after the application of the assets of the decedent's estate. A proceeding to assert this liability may not be commenced unless the personal representative has received a written demand by a surviving spouse, a creditor, or one acting for a minor dependent child of the decedent, and a proceeding may not be commenced later than two years following the death of the decedent. A beneficiary against whom the proceeding is brought may elect to transfer to the personal representative the security registered in the name of the beneficiary after the death of the deceased owner if the beneficiary still owns the security, or the net proceeds received by the beneficiary upon disposition of the security by the beneficiary, and that transfer fully discharges the beneficiary from all liability under this subdivision. Amounts or securities recovered by the personal representative must be administered as part of the deceased owner's estate.</p>
          <p>This subdivision does not affect the right of a registering entity to register a security in the name of the beneficiary, or make a registering entity liable to the estate of a deceased owner, except for a reregistration after a registering entity has received written notice from any claimant to an interest in the security objecting to implementation of a registration in beneficiary form.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-307.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.7.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 7</a>,8</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-308">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-308 PROTECTION OF REGISTERING ENTITY.</h1>
        <p>(a) A registering entity is not required to offer or to accept a request for security registration in beneficiary form. If a registration in beneficiary form is offered by a registering entity, the owner requesting registration in beneficiary form assents to the protections given to the registering entity by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a>.</p>
        <p>(b) By accepting a request for registration of a security in beneficiary form, the registering entity agrees that the registration will be implemented on death of the deceased owner as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a>.</p>
        <p>(c) A registering entity is discharged from all claims to a security by the estate, creditors, heirs, or devisees of a deceased owner if it registers a transfer of the security in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-307">524.6-307</a> and does so in good faith reliance (i) on the registration, (ii) on sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a>, and (iii) on information provided to it by affidavit of the personal representative of the deceased owner, or by the surviving beneficiary or by the surviving beneficiary's representatives, or other information available to the registering entity. The protections of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a> do not extend to a reregistration or payment made after a registering entity has received written notice from any claimant to any interest in the security objecting to implementation of a registration in beneficiary form. No other notice or other information available to the registering entity affects its right to protection under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a>.</p>
        <p>(d) The protection provided by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a> to the registering entity of a security does not affect the rights of beneficiaries in disputes between themselves and other claimants to ownership of the security transferred or its value or proceeds.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-308.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.9.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-309">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-309 NONTESTAMENTARY TRANSFER; REVOCATION OF DESIGNATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.524.6-309.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/524/full#stat.524.6-309.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Nontestamentary transfer on death.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A transfer on death resulting from a registration in beneficiary form is effective by reason of the contract regarding the registration between the owner and the registering entity and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a> and is not testamentary.</p>
          <p>(b) Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a> do not limit the rights of creditors of security owners against beneficiaries and other transferees under other laws of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.524.6-309.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/524/full#stat.524.6-309.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Revocation of beneficiary designation by will.</span></h2>
          <p>A registration in beneficiary form may be canceled by specific reference to the security or the securities account in the will of the sole owner or the last to die of multiple owners, but the terms of the revocation are not binding on the registering entity unless it has received written notice from any claimant to an interest in the security objecting to implementation of a registration in beneficiary form prior to the registering entity reregistering the security. If the beneficiary designation is canceled, the security belongs to the estate of the deceased sole owner or the estate or the last to die of all multiple owners.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-309.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.10.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 10</a>,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-310">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-310 TERMS, CONDITIONS, AND FORMS FOR REGISTRATION.</h1>
        <p>(a) A registering entity offering to accept registrations in beneficiary form may establish the terms and conditions under which it will receive requests (i) for registrations in beneficiary form, and (ii) for implementation of registrations in beneficiary form, including requests for cancellation of previously registered TOD beneficiary designations and requests for reregistration to effect a change of beneficiary. The terms and conditions so established may provide for proving death, avoiding or resolving any problems concerning fractional shares, designating primary and contingent beneficiaries, and substituting a named beneficiary's descendants to take in the place of the named beneficiary in the event of the beneficiary's death. Substitution may be indicated by appending to the name of the primary beneficiary the letters LDPS, standing for "lineal descendants per stirpes." This designation substitutes a deceased beneficiary's descendants who survive the owner for a beneficiary who fails to so survive, the descendants to be identified and to share in accordance with the law of the beneficiary's domicile at the owner's death governing inheritance by descendants of an intestate. Other forms of identifying beneficiaries who are to take on one or more contingencies, and rules for providing proofs and assurances needed to satisfy reasonable concerns by registering entities regarding conditions and identities relevant to accurate implementation of registrations in beneficiary form, may be contained in a registering entity's terms and conditions.</p>
        <p>(b) The following are illustrations of registrations in beneficiary form which a registering entity may authorize:</p>
        <p>(1) Sole owner-sole beneficiary: John S. Brown TOD (or POD) John S. Brown Jr.</p>
        <p>(2) Multiple owners-sole beneficiary: John S. Brown Mary B. Brown JT TEN TOD John S. Brown Jr.</p>
        <p>(3) Multiple owners-primary and secondary (substituted) beneficiaries: John S. Brown Mary B. Brown JT TEN TOD John S. Brown Jr. SUB BENE Peter Q. Brown or John S. Brown Mary B. Brown JT TEN TOD John S. Brown Jr. LDPS.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-310.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.12.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 12</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.6-311">
        <h1 class="shn">524.6-311 APPLICATION.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-301">524.6-301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.6-311">524.6-311</a> apply to registrations of securities in beneficiary form made before, on, or after June 1, 1992, by decedents dying on or after June 1, 1992.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.6-311.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/461/#laws.2.13.0">1992 c 461 art 2 s 13</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">Article 8<br>EFFECTIVE DATE AND REPEALER</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.8-101">
        <h1 class="shn">524.8-101 PROVISIONS FOR TRANSITION.</h1>
        <p>Except as provided elsewhere in this chapter, on the effective date of this chapter:</p>
        <p>(1) the chapter applies to any wills of decedents dying thereafter;</p>
        <p>(2) the chapter applies to any proceedings in court then pending or thereafter commenced regardless of the time of the death of decedent except to the extent that in the opinion of the court the former procedure should be made applicable in a particular case in the interest of justice or because of infeasibility of application of the procedure of this chapter;</p>
        <p>(3) every personal representative including a person administering an estate of a minor or incompetent holding an appointment on that date, continues to hold the appointment but has only the powers conferred by this chapter and is subject to the duties imposed with respect to any act occurring or done thereafter;</p>
        <p>(4) an act done before the effective date in any proceeding and any accrued right is not impaired by this chapter. If a right is acquired, extinguished or barred upon the expiration of a prescribed period of time which has commenced to run by the provisions of any statute before the effective date, the provisions shall remain in force with respect to that right;</p>
        <p>(5) any rule of construction or presumption provided in this chapter applies to instruments executed and multiple party accounts opened before the effective date unless there is a clear indication of a contrary intent.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.8-101.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/442/#laws.8.524.0">1974 c 442 art 8 s 524</a>.8-101; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/347/#laws.0.80.0">1975 c 347 s 80</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.524.8-102"><b>524.8-102</b> [Obsolete, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/442/#laws.8.524.0">1974 c 442 art 8 s 524</a>.8-102]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.524.8-103">
        <h1 class="shn">524.8-103 EARLY EFFECTIVE DATE.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.8-101">524.8-101</a>, the provisions of Laws 1974, chapter 442 relating to bonds found at sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.3-603">524.3-603</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.3-606">524.3-606</a> and Laws 1974, chapter 442, article 9, and that portion of Laws 1974, chapter 442, article 8, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.8-102">524.8-102,</a> which repeals Minnesota Statutes 1971, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/525.32">525.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/525.324">525.324</a>, are effective August 1, 1974.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.524.8-103.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/442/#laws.8.524.0">1974 c 442 art 8 s 524</a>.8-103; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/86/#laws.1.79.0">2009 c 86 art 1 s 79</a></p>
      </div>
    </div>
